## Document 055: 2026年10月PMI数据点评：季节性因素影响下制造业PMI回落

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月PMI数据点评：季节性因素影响下制造业PMI回落.pdf
Report source: 开源证券
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

事件点评 
请务必参阅正文后面的信息披露和法律声明 1 / 5

2026年 11月 02日

《工业企业营收、利润延续高位增长
—2026 年 9 月工业企业利润点评》
-2026.10.28 
《财政支出增速有所改善—2026年 9
月财政数据点评》-2026.10.27 
《对央行的政策利率择机切换到隔夜
的理解—事件点评》-2026.10.22  
季节性因素影响下制造业 PMI回落 
——2026年 10月 PMI数据点评

陈曦（分析师） 王帅中（联系人）  
chenxi2@kysec.cn 
证书编号：S0790521100002 
wangshuaizhong@kysec.cn 
证书编号：S0790125070016

事件：国家统计局公布 2026年 10月采购经理指数运行情况，制造业 PMI为 49.3%
（前值为 50.1%，下同），环比下降 0.8pct；非制造业 PMI为 49.7%（50.2%），
环比下降 0.5pct；综合 PMI为 49.7%（50.7%），环比下降 1.0pct。 
⚫ 10月 PMI数据关注点 
受十一假期影响，10月为传统制造业淡季，制造业 PMI回落至荣枯线以下。制
造业 PMI为 49.3%，环比下降 0.8pct，若观察均值，2021-2025年 10月制造业
PMI均值为 49.4%，环比均值为-0.5pct。但需要注意的是，2021-2025年中仅有
2024年 10月制造业 PMI较 9月回升， 其他年份均为回落，2024年 10月是 “924”
出台增量政策影响下的第一个月，因此环比回升，观察其他年份的变化， 2022
年 10月制造业 PMI环比回落 0.9pct，2023年回落 0.7pct，2025年回落 0.8pct，
本次回落幅度基本符合季节性水平。 
制造业 PMI不及市场预期，Wind统计的 3家机构预测中位数为 50.0%，平均数
为 50.0%。一方面是受假期错位影响，中秋节假期落在 9月，而 2025年中秋节
假期与国庆假期相连位于 10月，部分生产提前于 9月释放，另一方面或与 10
月国际油价中枢不断抬升，原材料成本高涨下对生产端的抑制有关。 
从分类指数看，制造业生产端与需求端同步回落导致制造业PMI回落，10月生
产回落速度快于需求，海外需求回落至收缩区间。生产指数环比下降 1.9pct、新
订单指数环比下降 1.1pct、新出口订单指数环比下降 1.3pct。新出口订单回落至
收缩区间，除过受假期因素影响外，或与近期海外科技股调整下 AI景气有所下
降相关。本次生产端与需求端同步回落，但生产端回落幅度更大，主要受假期因
素影响，对生产活动产生扰动，而新订单指数同样回落到收缩区间，一定程度上
反应当前内需仍偏弱，需要持续观察政策落地后的传导效果。生产经营活动预期
指数为 53.4%，环比下降 0.4pct，制造业企业对后续市场信心保持稳定。 
分行业来看，“K”型分化特征仍较为明显，新动能保持较快增长。非制造业新订
单指数为 44.4%，环比下降 2.1pct，回落至 45%水平以下，且已连续 42个月处
于收缩区间。从行业上看，四大行业 PMI较前值均有所回落，高技术制造业与
装备制造业 PMI仍处扩张区间，相关行业企业生产经营保持扩张，环比分别下
降 1.2pct和 0.4pct，消费品行业和基础原材料行业PMI处于收缩区间，环比分别
下降 1.9pct和 0.7pct。建筑业、服务业新订单指数均处于收缩区间，短期内市场
内需不足或将持续，需求的提振仍需观察政策落地后的传导效果。 
建筑业、 服务业PMI环比均有所下降， 非制造业PMI为 49.7%， 环比下降0.5pct，
非制造业景气水平回落至收缩区间。建筑业 PMI为 48.0%，较前值下降 2.3pct；
服务业 PMI为 50.0%，较前值下降 0.2pct。建筑业业务活动预期指数为 52.3%，
环比下降 3.1pct，仍处扩张区间，行业仍对后续发展的预期保持稳定，近期地产
政策频出，对需求端有所提振，从经济数据来看，地产投资仍在持续寻底。 
⚫ 债市观点：债券收益率或将上行，警惕债市的反转风险 
对于债市，我们认为债券收益率或将上行，警惕债市的反转风险。 
第一，市场大多观点认为，后续 DR001将围绕 1.25%运行，这存在落空的可能
性，或有利多出尽的风险。 
第二，金融时报连发两文指出“央行研究切换公开市场操作主力品种，属于货币
政策框架改革的一项举措，不同于通常讲的货币政策降息加息…市场各方要避免
将央行操作机制改革举措当作降息来炒作”，或指明监管对当前债市的态度趋于
谨慎。 
第三，拥挤度层面，当前公募等非银机构债券久期已达近年高位，特别是经过这
轮换锚发酵后，拥挤度再次提升，当前存在拥挤度调整、估值回调风险。 
第四，基本面层面，从 9月经济数据来看，国内经济有触底回升迹象，9月社融
同比 2025年 9月正增，政府债发行速度加快，政策逐渐落地下经济动能边际修
复，当前债市的定价尚未反应基本面的边际修复，这也是潜在的风险。 
⚫ 风险提示：政策变化超预期；经济变化超预期。

度，独立、客观地出具本报告，并对内容和观点负责。本报告清晰准确地反映了署名人员的研究观点，所包含的分
析基于各种假设，不同假设可能导致分析结果出现重大不同。本报告采用的各种估值方法及模型均有其局限性，估
值结果不保证所涉及证券能够在该价格交易。本报告署名人员保证他们报酬的任何一部分不曾与，不与，也将不会

评级 说明
证券评级
买入（Buy） 预计相对强于市场表现 20%以上；
增持（outperform） 预计相对强于市场表现 5%～20%；
中性（Neutral） 预计相对市场表现在－5%～＋5%之间波动；
减持（underperform） 预计相对弱于市场表现 5%以下。
行业评级
看好（overweight） 预计行业超越整体市场表现；
中性（Neutral） 预计行业与整体市场表现基本持平；
看淡（underperform） 预计行业弱于整体市场表现。
备注：评级标准为以报告日后的 6~12个月内，证券相对于市场基准指数的涨跌幅表现，其中A股基准指数为沪
深 300指数、港股基准指数为恒生指数、新三板基准指数为三板成指（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针
对做市转让标的） 、美股基准指数为标普500或纳斯达克综合指数。我们在此提醒您，不同证券研究机构采用不同
的评级术语及评级标准。我们采用的是相对评级体系，表示投资的相对比重建议；投资者买入或者卖出证券的决

定取决于个人的实际情况，比如当前的持仓结构以及其他需要考虑的因素。投资者应阅读整篇报告，以获取比较
完整的观点与信息，不应仅仅依靠投资评级来推断结论。

## Document 064: 2026年7-9月期GDP予測（1次速報） ～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年7-9月期GDP予測（1次速報） ～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Indicators

2026 年 10 月30 日

1/3 
2026 年 7-9 月期 GDP 予測（1 次速報） 
～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～ 
経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト 新家義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

小幅ながら４四半期連続のプラス成長を予想、景気回復基調は維持 
11 月16 日に公表される2026 年７-９月期の実質ＧＤＰ成長率を前期比年率＋0.4％（前期比＋
0.1％）と予測する。４四半期連続のプラス成長になるだろう1。 
物価高による下押しや耐久財需要の反動に加え、天候不順の影響もあって個人消費が減少する
一方、設備投資が高い伸びになることが下支えになる見込みである。設備投資の伸びには特殊要
因も一部含まれているが、それを除いても堅調だったと考えられる。個人消費が弱含むなかでも
プラス成長を維持するとみられることは、企業部門の底堅さが景気を下支えしていることを示し
ている。過去３四半期に比較的高い成長が続いた後であることも踏まえれば、今回の減速をもっ
て景気の基調が弱まったと判断する必要はないだろう。家計部門の弱さは懸念材料だが、企業部
門の底堅さは維持されており、景気は引き続き緩やかな回復基調にあると評価できる。

輸入増による成長率押し下げと公的在庫のプラス寄与は表裏一体 
７-９月期のＧＤＰを評価する際には、原油輸入と公的在庫の動きをセットで捉える必要があ
る。４-６月期は、中東情勢の悪化によって原油輸入が激減したことが、ＧＤＰ統計上は輸入減を
通じて成長率を押し上げた。一方、輸入の減少による国内供給の不足を補うために国家備蓄原油
が放出され、公的在庫の取り崩しが進んだ。この結果、公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度▲0.5％Pt
（前期比年率寄与度▲2.1％Pt）と、成長率を大きく押し下げた。輸入減による成長率の押し上げ
は、公的在庫の減少と表裏一体の関係にあった。 
７-９月期は、これと逆の動きが生じるとみられる。政府・民間による代替調達の進展によって
原油輸入が回復するため、統計上は輸入増が成長率の押し下げ要因になる。一方、輸入の回復に
伴って国家備蓄原油の取り崩しは一服しており、公的在庫の減少幅は前期に比べて大きく縮小す
るとみられる。この結果、公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度＋0.4％Pt（前期比年率寄与度＋1.5％
Pt）と、成長率を大きく押し上げることが予想される。

1 10 月 30 日時点で入手可能な経済指標を元に作成している。国際収支や家計調査等、今後公表される経済指標の結果
を踏まえて予測値を修正する可能性がある。

### Page 2

2/3 
輸入増による成長率の押し下げと公的在庫変動による押し上げは、ともに原油の供給環境が改
善したことに伴う動きである。両者を個別に景気の強弱として評価することは適切ではなく、一
体として見るべきだろう。 
むしろ重要なのは、代替調達の進展によって国家備蓄への依存を減らしながら必要な原油を確
保できる状況になり、資源不足が生産活動を大きく制約するリスクが低下していることである。
ＧＤＰ統計上、輸入の増加は成長率の押し下げ要因となるものの、経済実態としては国内で利用
可能な資源の供給回復を意味している。この点は、７-９月期の日本経済を評価するうえで、むし
ろ前向きに捉えるべきだろう。

需要項目別の動向 
個人消費は前期比▲0.2％と小幅減少を予想する。８～９月に各地で相次いだ大雨や台風などの
天候不順が外出機会やサービス消費を抑制した可能性がある。また、４-６月期には自動車やエア
コンなどの耐久財販売が増加したが、７-９月期にはこうした一時的な押し上げ効果が剥落した。
加えて、物価高による節約志向が根強いなか、食料品や日用品などの非耐久財消費にも弱さがみ
られる。高い賃上げや底堅い雇用環境は下支え要因となっているものの、天候不順という一時的
な要因もあって個人消費は総じて力強さを欠く結果になったとみられる。 
設備投資は前期比＋2.0％と、高い伸びを予想する。４-６月期には特許権の海外譲渡に伴う研
究開発投資の減少という特殊要因があったが、７-９月期はその影響が剥落することが押し上げ要
因になった面はある。もっとも、こうした特殊要因を除いても設備投資は明確に増加したと考え
られ、底堅い動きとなっている。企業収益が高水準を維持するなか、人手不足に対応した省力化
投資やデジタル・ＡＩ関連投資への需要は根強く、企業の設備投資意欲は引き続き強い。設備投
資計画も高い伸びとなっており、設備投資は均してみれば緩やかな増加基調にあると判断してよ
いだろう。 
なお、特許権の海外への譲渡により、テクニカルに４-６月期はサービス輸出が上振れ、設備投
資が下振れたが、７-９月期はその剥落によりサービス輸出が下振れ、設備投資が上振れとなる見
込みである。両者は相殺し合うため、成長率全体でみれば概ねニュートラルとなる。 
輸出は前期比＋1.2％と４四半期連続の増加を予想する。４-６月期に大口要因によって研究開
発サービスの受取が急増した分が剥落することで、サービス輸出が減少する一方、財輸出が高い
伸びとなり、財貨・サービス輸出全体では増加を維持したとみられる。半導体製造装置などの資
本財輸出が好調だった。地域別では、ＥＵ向けが低調だった一方、ＡＳＥＡＮ、ＮＩＥｓ向けの
資本財輸出が伸びている。また、４-６月期に急減した中東向け輸出が持ち直したことも押し上げ
につながっている。 
輸入は前期比＋3.3％と大幅な増加が予想される。４-６月期には資源輸入の激減により輸入が
大幅に減少していたが、代替調達の進展により７-９月期は明確に持ち直した。この結果、外需寄
与度は前期比▲0.3％Pt（前期比年率▲1.3％Pt）になったと予想される。 
ただし、この外需寄与度のマイナスは、資源輸入の回復に伴う輸入の増加によって生じるもの

込む。

Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Momentum Seen Sustained into
2027
SL T, photonics (transceiver+CPO), and power testing should
continue to drive growth into 2027, with contributions from FT
handlers and burn-in ovens likely emerging as well. Stay OW.
3Q26 miss on GM: See details in Results Summary
Growth momentum seen sustained into 4Q26 and 2027: Management indicated
that the strong business momentum observed in 3Q26 is likely to stay intact going

forward, driven by both power testing and semi & photonics businesses. Normalized
GM remains at ~60% (2026 guidance of ~60% GM implies GM back to ~60% in
4Q26 vs. 57.5% in 3Q26).
• Power testing: 60-70% of revenue is AI-related for 2026, and it is expected
to be sustained into 2027. HVDC testing tools could be the major driver, and
it has already received orders from customers.
• SLT: 9M26 revenue doubled on a YoY basis. SL T demand used to focus on

one major customer, but management indicated that it has added AVGO and
MRVL as new customers. Moreover, demand in 4Q26/1H27 should be mostly
related to inference applications, including CPU/ASIC/LPU.
• Photonics: 9M26 revenue grew by 4x on  a YoY basis. Demand from
transceivers should remain solid and CPO should grow notably in 2027.
Chroma focuses on Insertion 3/4o and shipments for the mass production
line next year  are seen reaching 4-5x vs. that for the pilot line this year.

Moreover, it has added another customer for a pilot run in 9M27 and mass
production in 4Q27. At the industry level, if insertion is excluded from mass
production, Insertion 3 tool demand should be higher. Opportunities will
likely be much larger when CPO penetrates the scale-up network.
• New businesses: FT handlers have been qualified by multiple HPC/CSP
customers and orders have been placed. Burn-in ovens have secured the first
CSP customer, with more HPC customers expected to follow next year.

These should be new addressable markets for Chroma in the coming years.
Stay OW: We believe Chroma should continue to benefit from the AI compute/
networking/power testing opportunities due to its strong customer relationship,
solid execution and outstanding technology expertise. Our PT of NT$2,800 is based
on 40x 2027e P/E, implying a PEG of 0.6 (vs. 1.0 over the past decade).

3Q26 miss on GM:
Off revenue of  NT$17,926mn (+32% QoQ/+180% YoY), GM contracted 3ppt QoQ, to
57.5%, below our estimate at 60.5% and consensus at 62.7%, mainly owing to a less
favorable product mix.
Opex of NT$3,141mn (opex ratio 17.5%) was lower than our estimate at NT$3,797mn (opex
ratio 21.2%) and consensus at NT$3,321mn (opex ratio 21.7%), reflecting operating
leverage.
This led to operating profit of NT$7,167mn (OPM up 1.2ppt QoQ, to 40.0%), 2% higher

than our estimate at NT$7,040mn (OPM 39.3%) and 14% higher than consensus at NT
$6,281mn (OPM 41.0%).
With a narrower non-op gain of NT$186mn, net profit reached NT$5,647mn (EPS NT
$13.37), 2% short of our estimate at NT$5,790mn (EPS NT$13.71) and 6% short of
consensus at NT$5,317mn (EPS NT$12.59).

A Closer Look
Test instrument and ATS segment looks set to reach another
record high in 2026
Revenue for the segment increased 55% YoY to NT$10,546mn (37% of the mix) in 2025.
We estimate it will rise 118% YoY to NT$24,564mn (40% of the mix) in 2026. The ever-
increasing power consumption of AI clusters should continue to drive demand for higher
spec testing tools. Along with our forecast of a 75% CAGR for global power supply unit

demand in the coming three years (see our report), this should remain a major driver for
Chroma, in our view.

Multiple drivers in the semiconductor business
System-level testing  (SL T) business
SL T should benefit from the capex cycle of NVIDIA's Rubin generation GPU, AMD's MI450
GPU and more ASIC accelerators.  Management pointed out that discrete testing tools are
still preferred  by major customers.  We are also seeing more HPC chip designers adopting

the system level testing process to make sure defects won't be incorporated into the AI
systems. We believe Chroma, as the first mover in this market, should benefit from
increasing chip volume, as well as higher TDP.
Metrology business
The metrology business should continue to benefit from current advanced packaging
architectures from its foundry and OSAT customers. It is confident that the performance
of its tools can win future architecture projects, which our industry checks suggest should

have higher metrology density.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
Exhibit 4: Chroma –  Offerings for Metrology in Advanced Packaging
Source: Chroma
Chroma's metrology equipment employs white light interferometry technology. The basic
working principle is that white light is emitted on the reference mirror and the sample,
which are placed at the same distance from the light source. The reflected beams from
the reference mirror and sample surface are then received by a light sensor.
Interference occurs when two light beams collide, which could cause  them to either

strengthen or weaken, depending on the phase of the two light beams. Thus, by
measuring the intensity of the combined light beams, the distance between light sensor
and multiple points on the target surface can be calculated through an algorithm. This can
then be used to measure the dimensions of the structure or the alignment of layers.
Exhibit 5: Concept of Light Interference
Source: Keyence
Exhibit 6: Setup of Interferometer
Source: Wikipedia
CPO contribution to start from 2026

Chroma has been qualified for optical engine testing in Insertion 3 and system-level
testing in Insertion 4, and there might be incremental opportunities if supply chain players'
progress falls behind for testing equipment in Insertion 2. Over the longer term, Chroma
also  aspires to participate in the PIC optical/electrical/reliability testing in Insertion 1.

FT handlers to add another revenue stream
In HPC testing, Chroma used to focus mostly on the SL T side, although it did unveil its first

believe this is a natural extension of its expertise and customer relationships accumulated
in SL Ts. We expect Chroma to gain more traction in FT handlers for AI HPCs in 2027.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 40x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 40x is justified by the 68% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

## Document 135: 261030_daiwa_kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/261030_daiwa_kinsus.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Kinsus reported 3Q26 results on 30 October. Net income came in at 
TWD2.39bn (EPS: TWD4.68), 8% below our estimate (TWD2.59bn) but 13% above the 
Bloomberg consensus (TWD2.12bn). Operating results were stronger than expected, 
driven mainly by an improving product mix, including a rising contribution from Nvidia CPU-
related products. While 3Q26 revenue of TWD14.54bn (up 16% QoQ and 40% YoY) was 
largely in line with expectations, gross margin reached 33.9%, 0.7ppt/0.9ppt above our 
estimate/the Bloomberg consensus. Operating margin reached 22.1%, versus our 20.5% 
estimate and the street’s 19.4%, on gross-margin expansion and higher operating 
leverage. However, weaker-than-expected non-operating profit and a higher minority 
interest (TWD373m) versus our estimate (TWD311m) resulted in the 8% net-profit miss 
(vs. our expectations).

Looking ahead, we expect 4Q26 revenue to increase 10% QoQ, driven mainly by ongoing 
price increases and product-mix optimisation, with further gross-margin expansion. We 
believe AMD’s new CPU platform (i.e., Venice) and BT substrates for optical transceivers 
could be the next growth drivers for Kinsus in 2027. Separately, Kinsus’s board approved 
an additional c.TWD60bn project budget for capacity expansion on 30 October, which we 
view as further evidence of tight supply conditions and improving demand visibility. We 
have a Buy (1) rating on Kinsus with a 12M TP of TWD1,580, based on 36x our 1-year-
forward EPS forecast. Key downside risks: weaker-than-expected demand for memory, 
PCs, gaming GPUs or AI servers.

Kinsus’s 3Q26 results highlights  
(TWDm) Actual QoQ% YoY% Daiwa Diff % Consensus Diff % 
Revenue 14,540 16% 40% 14,539 0% 14,468 0% 
Gross profit 4,935 51% 151% 4,824 2% 4,779 3% 
  Gross margin (%) 33.9%   33.2%  33.0%  
Operating profit 3,216 92% 412% 2,983 8% 2,812 14%

Source: Company data, Daiwa forecasts, Bloomberg

What’s the impact: 
Below is our first take on Kinsus’s 3Q26 results, released on 30 October.

• 3Q26 operating results beat expectations; net profit missed our estimate. Net 
income came in at TWD2.39bn (EPS: TWD4.68), 8% below our estimate (TWD2.59bn) 
but 13% above the Bloomberg consensus (TWD2.12bn). Operating results were 
stronger than expected, driven mainly by an improving product mix, including a rising 
contribution from Nvidia CPU-related products. While 3Q26 revenue of TWD14.54bn (up 
16% QoQ and 40% YoY) was largely in line with expectations, gross margin reached 
33.9%, 0.7ppt/0.9ppt above our estimate/the Bloomberg consensus. Operating margin 
reached 22.1%, versus our 20.5% estimate and the street’s 19.4%, on gross-margin 
expansion and higher operating leverage. However, non-operating profit of TWD36m 
was 59%/72% below our/Bloomberg consensus forecasts, while higher-than-expected 
minority interest of TWD373m (vs. our TWD311m estimate) resulted in the 8% net-profit 
miss (vs. our expectations).

AMD’s new CPU platform (i.e., Venice) and BT substrates for optical transceivers could 
be the next growth drivers for Kinsus in 2027. Separately, Kinsus’s board approved an 
additional c.TWD59.8bn project budget for capacity expansion on 30 October. This 
follows two project budgets of TWD23.5bn and TWD19.6bn announced in January and 
July 2026, respectively. The three projects would represent an aggregate approved 
budget of c.TWD102.9bn. We view the latest expansion plan as further evidence of tight 
supply conditions and stronger demand visibility.

What we recommend: 
We have a Buy (1) rating on Kinsus with a 12M TP of TWD1,580, based on a target PER 
of 36x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key downside risk: weaker-than-
expected demand for memory, PCs, gaming GPUs or AI servers.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

### Page 1

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
What's new? Earlier today, a fire broke out at Panasonic's (covered by Kazuo
Yoshikawa) CCL factory in Hsinchu, Taiwan. The fire was extinguished before noon
(see Reuters' report), but Panasonic has yet to issue an official statement in
response to the incident.
Our view: Our checks indicate that this plant has monthly capacity of ~200K sheets

and primarily produces M6/7 and some lower-end BT substrate CCLs. Panasonic also
operates CCL sites in Fukushima, Japan; Guangzhou and Suzhou, China; and
Ayutthaya, Thailand, which remains under construction. Most high-end M7/8 CCL is
produced in Fukushima, while the China sites supply much of its mainstream CCL.
Our supply chain checks suggest Panasonic is reallocating capacity toward high-end
CCL for AI applications – particularly a key US hyperscaler’s in-house ASICs, as high-

end CCL supply remains tight. Although the Hsinchu plant is immaterial to group
capacity, the fire could accelerate this shift, potentially driving near-term customer
order overflow to Taiwanese CCL competitors.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
A fire at Panasonic's CCL factory in Hsinchu may push near-term orderflow to
Taiwanese competitors.
Within our coverage, we see EMC as most likely to receive orders, followed by
TUC and Shengyi Tech (EW).
EMC is also a high-end CCL supplier to Panasonic’s key ASIC customer.
Valuations are attractive for EMC and TUC, and we remain OW on both.

November 4, 2026 04:01 PM GMT

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

營收走勢圖

研究員/莊佳蓉 EVA8894122@uni-psg.com

雖考量 VR 整體遞延加上中系手機客戶拉貨趨緩，研究部微幅下修 27
年預估，但仍然維持富世達買進投資建議，主係：(1)3Q26 伺服器營收
占比大幅提升至 81%，帶動毛利率達 38.0%，創歷史新高並優於預期；
(2)QD 月產能已擴充至 450 萬顆 （+50%） ， 且2027 年持續擴產 ；(3)2027
年受惠 VR 放量，加上CSP ASIC 專案陸續貢獻，營運成長性高；(4)目
前評價已回落至 17.7 倍，位於低檔。綜上所述，研究部下修 2027 年
營收至 295 億元 （原估316 億元） ，EPS 下修至 121.74 元 （原估124.48
元），給予 24 倍本益比，目標價下調至 2,920 元。

重點摘要： 
1. 產品組合大幅優化，3Q26毛利率躍升至38.0% 
3Q26營收45億元，季增20.4%，年增26.3%，符合預期。其中：
(1)3Q成長主要來自伺服器QD，季增將近8成，主因GB300持續
出貨，加上VR200小量出貨；(2)中系手機部分，受記憶體漲價
及缺貨影響，終端拉貨力道疲弱，單季營收季減51%；(3)滑軌
部分，因通用伺服器記憶體缺料影響，季減將近4成。整體而
言，因QD營收大幅提升，伺服器營收占比由2Q的55%大幅提升
至3Q的81%，產品組合轉佳，故毛利率達38.0%（原估37.0%），
季增4.0ppts，優於預期。營益率32.4%（原估32.4%） ，季增
4.5ppts，符合預期；EPS達18.11元（原估17.57元） ，優於預
期。

2. 4Q26營收微幅下修，惟毛利率續創新高 
展望4Q26，考量中系手機客戶雖為旺季， 但受缺料及漲價影響 ，
拉貨態度偏保守。在VR部分，根據供應鏈調查，若以L11機櫃
組裝端來看，4Q26~1Q27僅小量出貨，真正放量須至2Q27，時
程遞延。但若以零組件端看，液冷QD屬於偏L6端的產品，出貨
時程較早，4Q已開始出貨。但考量放量時程及手機影響，研究
部微幅下修4Q26營收至51億元 （原估53億元） ，季增12.7%，年
增27.6%。毛利率部分，考量伺服器占比可望維持在高檔，上
修毛利率至38.6%（原估37.3%） ，季增0.6ppts；EPS微幅下修
至20.42元（原估20.89元）。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. NV、ASIC雙引擎並進 
基本面仍維持正向看待，QD成長動能明確：(1)NV VR 4Q逐漸
出貨，動能延續至 27年；下一代Ultra設計將導入更高流量
ZQD(單價翻倍)， 促進價值提升；(2)AWS T3已配合客戶時程出
貨，新世代平台水冷滲透率進一步提升；(3)其他ASIC專案陸
續進入量產，本次新增MSFT Maia QD，預估單櫃約576顆(32顆
*18層)，Google TPU、Meta也陸續出貨。產能方面，QD月產能
已擴充至450萬顆（+50%），2027年持續擴產，預期提升至650
萬顆，足以支撐後續訂單需求。

4. VR放量時程遞延，保守下修2027年營收 
然而 ，研究部仍考量以下因素 ， 保守微幅下修2027年營收預估 ：
(1)NV平台部分，考量VR延後至2Q27放量，恐連帶影響Rubin 
Ultra，且目前機櫃架構亦尚未定案，ZQD用量存在變數；(2)
中系手機客戶整體拉貨動能偏弱， 研究部預估明年衰退，故些
微下修27年預估。

5. 目前評價已回檔，潛在空間浮現，維持買進建議 
綜上所述 ， 研究部下修2026年營收至175億元 （原估178億元） ，
年增41.0%，惟毛利率上修至36.0%（原估35.4%），EPS上修至
64.30元 （原估64.20元）。同步下修2027年營收至295億元 （原
估316億元），EPS微幅下修至121.74元 （原估124.48元） 。目前
評價已回落至17.7倍，位於低檔，潛在空間已浮現，給予24倍
本益比，目標價由3,115元調整至2,920元，維持買進建議。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

## Document 220: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 - 기업

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
90 
SK하이닉스  (000660) 
2026.11.04

HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

업황 우려에 고유 할인까지 겹친 과도한 하락 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 230만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27 년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3배를 적용했다[표1]. 이는 상장 이후 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 해당한다. 
‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 102%, 66%, 47%로, ‘26년 정점 이후에도 과거 업사이클 
고점보다 높은 수준이 유지될 전망이다.  
한편, 이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해져 시장의 시야가 ‘28년으로 향할 경우 
‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 360만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 57% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -51%를 넘어섰고, 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
그러나 당사는 아래와 같은 투자포인트와 향후 주가 흐름을 제시한다.

‘28년 HBM 가치, 시가총액의 1.3배 
HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 cHBM으로 
갈수록 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 
물량과 가격이 결정된다. 동사는 이 전환이 업계에서 가장 선두권이다.  
당사는 동사 HBM 매출이 ‘26년 43조원에서 ‘28년 183조원으로 증가할 것으로 
전망한다. 만약, 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E(2 1배, 
15배, 1 2배)를 적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 47%, 93%, 135%를 
설명한다[표3]. ‘27 년에 이미 HBM 하나만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명된다는 
의미다. 시장이 통상적으로 받아들이는 멀티플 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 
다르지 않다.

150만원에 사면 ‘30년 EPS 2배 
동사의 주주환원은 자사주 매입/소각 중심일 것으로 상정한다. 당사는 동사가 현 
정책 기간(‘25~’27년)에 이어 ‘28년 이후에도 FCF의 50%를 환원하고 이 중 
70%를 자사주 매입/소각에 쓸 것으로 가정했다.  
당사 가정대로라면 총주주환원수익률은 현 시가총액 대비 ‘27년 14%, ‘28~’30년 
연 17~24%다[표4]. 
관건은 매입 단가다. 자사주 매입의 주당가치 개선 효과는 P/E가 낮을수록 
커지는데, 현 주가의 ‘27년 P/E는 2.7배다. 현 주가 수준인 150만원에 매년 균등 
매입한다고 ‘30년까지 주식수는 50% 줄어든다.   
그 결과 ‘30년 주당순이익은 매입이 없을 때 대비 2배가 된다[표4]. ‘28년 이후 
이익이 정체되더라도 주당 이익은 계속 늘어나는 구조이며, 이익 지속성에 대한 
의심이 주가를 누를수록 소각 효과는 커진다. 현 주가 수준의 정체가 장기화되기 
어려운 이유다.

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간이다. 솔리다임 상장 철회에 이어 3분기 
실적발표에서 ‘26년 주주환원 100조원과 매 분기 자사주 매입 방침이 제시되면서 환원 
의지는 검증되었다. 
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 불확실성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 가격 하락폭은 LTA 
방어력이 확인되기 전까지 블랙박스다. 이 불확실성이 멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 
계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 구간이 이어질 전망이다. 이 구간의 
기회비용은 환원으로 보상받는다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 신규 팹 물량이 소량 
출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 당사는 2H27에 ‘28년 HBM 협의와 
LTA 연장이 지속적으로 확인되면서 이익이 스트레스 테스트 범위 안에서 유지될 것으로 
전망하며[그림2], 이것이 숫자로 확인되는 시점에 순환 할인이 축소되고 재평가가 나타날 
것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.

 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상

## Document 224: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다실적에 수급을 더한다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다실적에 수급을 더한다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
여전히 가격 상승 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 95.8조원, 영업이익 75.1조원으로 예상한다. 
이전 전망 대비 감소한 수준이다. 원/달러 환율 영향이다. 영업이익률은 78.4%로 전 
분기 대비 2.1%p 상승할 전망이다. DRAM 매출액은 73.1조원, 영업이익 
59.2조원으로 추정한다. DRAM ASP는 이전 전망 대비 상향 조정했다. 일부 제품 
가격 인상을 반영한 영향이다. Bit Growth는 이전 전망 수준을 유지했다. ASP 
상향이 수익성 개선에 미친 영향은 크지 않을 전망이다. NAND 매출액은 22.4조원, 
영업이익은 15.9조원으로 예상한다. 
Global Top 수준 주주환원 규모/자사주 살 게 더 있다 
SK하이닉스는 2026년 8월 19일 주주환원에 대한 새로운 정책을 제시했다. FCF에서 
CAPEX를 제외한 금액의 50% 이상을 주주환원에 사용한다는게 핵심이다. 2026년 
기준으로 최소 90조원 수준일 것으로 추정한다. 자사주 중심으로 주주환원을 계획 
중이고 이미 집행 중인 40조원 이외에 4분기 중에 추가로 자사주 매입이 진행될 
것으로 예상한다. 배당도 일부 집행이 되겠지만 자사주가 중심이 될 전망이다. 
흔들리지 말아야 할 이유는 많다 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 모델의 성장으로 메모리에 
대한 수요가 지속적으로 높아지고 있고, 이에 비해 공급 증가는 제한적인 상황이 
상당기간 지속될 것으로 전망하기 때문이다. 이로 인해 현재 수준의 DRAM, NAND 
가격에 변동성은 낮고, 높은 수익성이 유지되면서 안정적 현금흐름 창출이 가능할 
것으로 전망한다. 현재 주가는 지나치게 저평가되어 있는 국면에 있다고 판단한다. 
목표주가는 12개월 예상 EPS 446,092원에 PER 9 배를 적용한 4,000,000원을 
유지한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

## Document 262: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
악조건 속에서도 이 정도 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 영업실적은 매출액 5조 7,536억원(yoy +30.2%, qoq 
`-9.1%), 영업이익 8,992억원(yoy +61.3%, qoq -13.5%)으로 당사 추정치에 대체로 
부합하였다. 조업일수 감소와 원화 강세로 매출액이 감소했으며, 임단협 타결로 
인한 성과금 및 격려금이 당분기에 비용으로 반영되었다. 해양플랜트 부문에서는 
RUYA 프로젝트의 공사 진행률 확대와 TRION 프로젝트의 C/O(계약 변경) 
180억원이 추가로 반영되며 매출액이 전분기대비 0.7% 증가하였다. 엔진기계 
부문은 환율 하락을 판가 상승과 D/F(이중연료) 비중 증가로 상쇄하며 전분기와 
유사한 영업실적을 달성했다. 
2027년에도 지속될 수주 시점 효과 
3분기 대형선 야드의 수주 시점별 매출 비중은 2022년 4%, 2023년 45%, 2024년 
31%, 2025년 20% 수준으로 파악된다. 상대적으로 선가가 낮았던 2022~2023년 
물량이 여전히 매출의 절반 가량을 차지하고 있는 셈이다. 따라서 2027년 말까지도 
고선가 물량의 매출 비중 확대가 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이는 LNG운반선 매출 
비중 감소에도 불구하고 수익성을 방어할 수 있는 핵심 요인으로 작용할 전망이다.   
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 550,000원으로 하향 
투자의견은 매수로 유지하나, 목표주가는 550,000원으로 34.5% 하향한다. 환율 
가정을 기존 1,450원 수준에서 1,400원으로 조정함에 따라 목표주가 산정의 기준이 
되는 2027~2028년 실적추정치를 하향조정하였다. 또한 미국 네오 클라우드 
업체들의 자금 조달 여건 악화로 데이터센터 증설이 지연되고 있어, 발전용 엔진 
증설을 근거로 동사에 부여하였던 프리미엄을 제거하여 P/E 배수를 19.8배로 
하향조정했다. 그러나 본업인 상선 부문은 향후 고부가가치 가스선의 발주 강세를 
전망하기 때문에 투자의견은 매수로 유지한다.

## Document 275: [402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439

Source PDF: all report pdf/[402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

SK 스퀘어 (402340/KS) 
하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대 
예정보다 빠른 SK 하이닉스 자사주 매입 
SK스퀘어의 자회사인 SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 20일부터 11월 19일까지 자사주 40
조원(2,407만주, 3.3%)을 매입 예정이다. 9월 29일까지 1,715만주, 예정 매입수량 
대비 71.3%를 매입 완료하였다. 매입 초기 매일 65만주 매입하다가 9월 10일부터는 
60만주를 매입하고 있는데, 이러한 추세가 유지된다면 10월 중순경 자사주 매입이 마
무리될 예정이다. 자사주 매입 후 소각 예정으로 소각이 완료되면 SK스퀘어의 SK하아
닉스에 대한 지분율은 20.0%에서 20.7%로 상승하게 된다.

자사주 매입 이후 추가적인 주주환원에 대한 기대 
SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 19일 정책기간(‘25년~’27년) 동안 발생하는 누적 FCF의 
50% 이상을 주주환원 하는 주주환원 정책을 발표한 바 있다. 26년 SK하이닉스의 예
상 FCF는 164조원(컨센서스 기준)으로 50%를 주주환원 할 경우 자사주 매입 금액
(40 조원)을 제외한 주주환원 재원은 42 조원이다. 잔여 주주환원 재원이 모두 배당으
로 지급된다면 SK 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 20.7%이므로 
SK스퀘어의 배당수입도 8.7조원에 달한다. 지난 3월 SK스퀘어는 ‘26년~’28년 경
상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 정책을 발표했다. 보수적으로 30%만 
가정하더라도 SK스퀘어의 배당재원은 2.6조원(현 시가총액 대비 1.8%)이다. SK스퀘
어는 연초 주주총회에서 배당가능이익 확보 차원에서 자본준비금 5 조 8,900억원을 
이익잉여금으로 전입한 바 있다. 이는 26년 재무제표가 확정되는 27년 3월 이후 배당
가능이익으로 편입되므로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 확대가 기대된다.

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 185 만원(유지) 
SK스퀘어의 순자산 가치 중 98% 이상이 SK하이닉스 지분가치이다. SK하이닉스는 
자사주 매입 이후 17.7% 주가가 상승했으나, SK 스퀘어 주가는 11.4% 상승했다. SK
하이닉스 자사주 매입·소각은 SK 스퀘어의 보유 지분가치 상승뿐 아니라 지분율도 상
승하기 때문에 자사주가 소각될 경우 SK스퀘어의 주가가 탄력적으로 움직일 가능성이 
높다. SK스퀘어 현재 NAV 대비 할인율은 43.7%, 12MF PBR 1.4배로 7월 이후 주
가 조정으로 밸류에이션 매력도 높아졌다. 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 185만원을 유지
한다.

2026-09-30

당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다.

투자판단 3 단계(6 개월기준) 15%이상 → 매수 / -15%~15% → 중립 / -15%미만 → 매도

## Document 285: AI军备竞赛烧钱不止，Meta拟发债至少200亿美元，期限最长30年

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI军备竞赛烧钱不止，Meta拟发债至少200亿美元，期限最长30年.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Meta 时隔六个⽉重返债券市场，启动五档美元债发⾏，期限覆盖 5 年⾄ 30 年，计划⾄少融
资 200 亿美元，预计还将发⾏欧元债券。随着 AI 基础设施投资持续扩张，科技巨头正加⼤债
务融资⼒度， AI 军备竞赛的资⾦压⼒开始向债市传导。
摘要：
Meta Platforms 正加⼤债务融资⼒度，为⼈⼯智能基础设施扩张筹集资⾦。
据彭博社 11 ⽉ 3 ⽇报道， Meta 已启动五档美元债券发⾏，期限从 5 年⾄ 30 年不等。这是 Meta 时隔
六个⽉再次发⾏债券，也使其成为最新⼀家在投资者对 AI 资本开⽀回报产⽣疑虑之际，仍⼤举进
⼊债券市场融资的科技巨头。
知情⼈⼠透露，此次美元债发⾏的 30 年期债券初始收益率指引为较美国国债⾼出约 2.5 个百分
点。此前彭博社报道称， Meta 计划通过此次债券发⾏⾄少筹集 200 亿美元，融资还预计将包括欧
元计价债券。
消息传出之际， Meta 美股盘前转跌。
报道称，此次美元债发⾏共分为五档，覆盖从中短期到超⻓期限的融资需求。  根据彭博披露的

发⾏信息，五档债券的初始利差指引分别为： 5 年期较美国国债⾼约 130 个基点， 7 年期⾼约 165
个基点， 10 年期⾼约 185 个基点， 20 年期⾼约 230 个基点， 30 年期⾼约 250 个基点。
此次债券发⾏的预期评级为穆迪 Aa3 、标普 AA- ，采⽤⾼级⽆抵押债券形式，募集资⾦将⽤于⼀
般公司⽤途。花旗集团和摩根⼠丹利担任联席账簿管理⼈，预计 11 ⽉ 5 ⽇结算。
Meta 此次融资的背后，是科技巨头 AI 基础设施投资持续扩张，以及资本市场融资⽅式的变化。
随着 AI 数据中⼼、服务器和算⼒基础设施建设不断推进，科技巨头正需要投⼊巨额资⾦。此前，
Meta 已宣布⼤幅增加 AI 相关资本开⽀，并通过股权和债务等多种渠道筹集资⾦。
但与过去依靠充沛经营现⾦流⽀撑投资不同， AI 基础设施建设的资⾦需求正在推动⼤型科技公司
更多转向债券市场。 Meta 此次计划⾄少融资 200 亿美元，且同时涉及美元和欧元债券，显⽰其融
资需求已不局限于单⼀市场。

## Document 339: EcoNews of 2 November 2026 (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/EcoNews of 2 November 2026 (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
5
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.40 / 2 November 2026ECONEWS
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
ADVANCED ECONOMIES
In brief. Data releases confirmed resilient to strong growth and
above-forecast inflation in most advanced economies, with France lag-
ging behind and earning a rating outlook downgrade from Fitch as a
result. Fed, ECB and BoJ remained on hold, as expected, and on track
for December hikes. Oil continues to fluctuate between escalation fears

and hopes for a return to the negotiating table. The tech sell-off in
equity markets intensified despite strong earnings, as markets balk at
large capex plans. This helped bond yields ease from recent highs.
EUROZONE
Resilience confirmed despite tightening credit. GDP expanded by a
stronger-than-expected 0.4% q/q in Q3 (0.2% ex-Ireland), with Spain,
Germany and the Netherlands contributing to the bulk of non-Irish
growth. We increase our growth forecast to 1.6% in 2027 (+0.1pp),

driven by further improving sentiment in manufacturing. Bank lending
conditions tightened further in Q3 for businesses, especially SMEs,
driven by France and Germany, while corporate and housing-loan de -
mand weakened. The ECB kept its deposit rate at 2.5% and signaled it
was firmly on track for a (likely final) December hike. President Lagarde
also confirmed she would step down early, on February 7, setting off
the succession race. EU leaders are expected to decide jointly on the

three Board members departing between January and May (President,
chief economist, and market operations), likely at their mid-December
meeting. Official candidates to succeed Lagarde are BIS Head Pablo
Hernandez de Cos and former Dutch central bank Governor Klaas Knot.
To watch next week: October flash CPI (Wednesday).
France: Another growth setback, with silver linings
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q in Q3 as a sharp drop in aeronauti -
cal production hit exports and investment. However, services out -

put grew by a robust 0.3% q/q, and household consumption also
grew more strongly than forecasted (+0.4%). While 2026 will ine -
vitably be knocked down (to 0.3%), we forecast 0.8% for 2027.
Nevertheless, Fitch issued an unscheduled rating outlook downgrade
to negative territory, on grounds that fiscal, growth and ad -
justment prospects have deteriorated since its August review.
A new review could be concluded as early as February. Harmonised
inflation accelerated to 3.8% y/y in October, with both energy and

non-energy contributing. Meanwhile, producer price inflation accele -
rated to 8.2% y/y in September. Insee’s index of household confidence
fell to 80, matching its 2013 all-time low, driven by a decline in ex -
pectations of its own financial situation. Private payroll employment
declined by 26,000 over Q3 (-0.1%). However, in previous quarters, the
decline in payroll employment was more than offset by the increase in
self-employment (not released until November 27).

To watch next week: September industrial production (Thursday); Sep-
tember foreign trade (Friday).
Germany: Growth holds up as inflation slows
GDP grew by 0.2% q/q in Q3, with consumption rising slightly but in -
vestment declining and export growth slowing. HICP inflation fell from
3.3% to 2.8% in October following energy-tax cuts and fuel discounts,
but core inflation also slowed, to 2.1%. Business confidence (Ifo survey)
reached its highest level in more than three years, led by manufactu -

ring, which is back in positive territory for the first time since 2023.
By contrast, consumer confidence deteriorated to its lowest since May,
due to growing concerns about inflation, which are weighing on consu-
mers’ spending intentions.
To watch next week: September retail sales (Monday); September fac-
tory orders (Thursday); September industrial production (Friday).
Italy: Stagnant growth meets energy-driven inflation surge
GDP was flat in Q3, missing expectations, as positive domestic demand

was offset by net exports. Economic sentiment improved, but HICP in -
flation jumped from 4.2% to 5.6% in October, its highest level since
September 2023, mainly because of regulated energy prices; core in -
flation also rose, to a more moderate 2%. More positively, economic
sentiment rose to 100.2 in October 2026 (from 99.4 in September),
despite the Eurozone decline. The improvement was driven by services
and construction. The Italian cabinet approved a budget focused on

middle-class tax cuts, financed partly by levies on banks, energy and
gambling companies. x
UNITED KINGDOM
A cautious budget buys breathing space. The budget offered 98 small
cost-of-living alleviation measures and £4.1 billion in household ener-
gy cost relief, while only modestly increasing taxes, including a tem -
porary rise in the bank surcharge. Its short-term stance is slightly ex -
pansionary, while major structural decisions on defence, pensions and

welfare have been postponed. The official budget watchdog, OBR, cut
its 2027 growth forecast to 1%, and firms’ inflation expectations conti-
nued to rise. UK gilts outperformed and equities advanced after the
budget. GBP rose to a six-week high against the dollar and a 19-month
high versus the euro as markets price a near-certain Bank of England
rate hike (to 4%) at this week meeting. We agree but, unlike market
pricing, we do not expect it to hike further.

To watch next week: Bank of England interest-rate decision (Thursday).
UNITED STATES
Fed awaiting more information despite strong growth. The Federal Re-
serve held its target range at 3.75–4.0%, with two members voting for a
25 bp increase, as Chair Warsh highlighted stronger growth, favourable
employment conditions and inflation that remains too high. Q3 GDP
accelerated to 3.4% annualised, powered by resilient consumption and
a 12.7% jump in business investment. Despite jobless claims remaining

near multi-decade lows, compensation costs adjusted for inflation de-
clined 0.4%, marking the second consecutive quarter in which labour
cost growth trailed price increases.
To watch next week: Midterm elections (Tuesday); October ISM ma -
nufacturing (Monday) and non-manufacturing (Wednesday); Q3 non-
farm productivity (Thursday); October non-farm payrolls (Friday).
JAPAN
BoJ on hold with underlying inflation “consistent with the 2% tar -
get”. The Bank of Japan kept its policy rate at 1.25%, with one dissent

in favour of a hike. Its outlook judged underlying inflation to be
consistent with the 2% target, a major shift in its assessment, although
Governor Ueda mentioned risks on both sides in his remarks, stres -
sing in particular the risk of inflation overshooting the target. It signals
continued gradual monetary adjustment, with a next hike expected in
December. Alongside the rate hold, the BOJ announced it would near -
ly triple the pace of ETF sales starting in January, to approximately

JPY 860 billion per year. Japanese equities shrugged off the announ -
cement.
To watch next week: Minutes of the September Bank of Japan meeting
(Thursday); September wage growth (Friday).

### Page 2

6
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.40 / 2 November 2026
The bank
for a changing

Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
EMERGING ECONOMIES
ASIA
China: Monetary policy reform and no growth signals
The PBOC disclosed a 1.25% overnight reverse-repo rate and an -
nounced that the overnight rate would become its principal policy
lever, starting 2nd November. It also pledged to develop cross-border
renminbi payments and introduced a liquidity backstop for non-bank

financial institutions. The 5th Plenum of the 20 th Central Committee of
the CCP focused on party discipline and crackdown on corrupt cadres,
without broader policy signals. Chinese equities had their worst month
since 2016, falling 8.5% in October, as doubts over AI earnings deepe -
ned investor pessimism. The PMIs published on 30 th October suggest
economic activity is losing momentum; in the manufacturing sector,
the RatingDog PMI fell slightly to 52 (from 52.1) while the official PMI

fell more sharply and dropped below 50 (49.3 vs. 50.1 in the previous
month).
South Korea and Taiwan: AI boom lifts growth even higher
In Korea, GDP grew by 1.0% q/q and 3.4% y/y in Q3, supported by boo -
ming semiconductor exports linked to global AI demand. The expansion
was uneven, however, as private-consumption growth slowed marke -
dly. In Taiwan, GDP surged by 3.2% q/q and 14.4% y/y in Q3, beating
expectations and accelerating from the previous quarter as technology

firms expanded capacity to meet strong demand.
EMEA
Poland: Lower inflation gives monetary policy some room
Headline inflation declined from 4.0% to 3.5% in October as electricity,
gas and transport fuel prices rose more slowly, helped by a temporary
fuel-price cap. Rates are likely to remain unchanged in the near term,
although renewed zloty weakness means a hike cannot be ruled out.
Saudi Arabia: Reserves fall as regional pressures intensify
Official reserve assets declined by 4.4% m/m in September to

SAR 1.75 tn, their lowest level since December, with foreign-currency
assets recording particularly sharp monthly and annual declines.
Türkiye: Pressure on lira and FX reserves
The lira headed for its largest monthly fall in 15 months and cen -
tral-bank reserves came under renewed pressure. This is the conse -
quence of the warning by MSCI that Türkiye could lose its emer -
ging-market status if measures to improve transparency and market
supervision following the Fund scandal are deemed insufficient.

LATIN AMERICA
Brazil’s post-election rout: stocks and real plunge, yields surge. Bra -
zilian markets, expecting a Flavio Bolsonaro victory, sold off sharply
after Luiz Inacio Lula da Silva won a fourth presidential term. The Ibo-
vespa fell 14% Monday — its largest daily drop since COVID-19 — and
ended the week down 18%. The real fell over 11% intraday and ended
the week down 8% against the euro. Meanwhile the yield on Brazil’s
10-year government bonds rose 250 bp to 15.5%, reaching its highest

level in over a decade. The selloff reflects fiscal concerns over pledges
to raise the minimum wage and Bolsa familia benefits and to offer free
public transport on weekends and bank holidays. Finance Minister Da-
rio Durigan’s pledge to seek a higher 2027 primary surplus, without a
new target, did little to reassure markets.
COMMODITIES
The oil market continues to fluctuate between escalation fears and
hopes for a return to the negotiating table. Brent prices were down by

5% over the week, reaching USD 109/b on Monday. Tensions on physical
supply continue to decline. Ditto for diesel crack spreads. On the back
of geopolitical tensions, lower-than-requested EU stock level and spe-
culation over a cold snap in Europe in November, European gas prices
(TTF) recorded another strong weekly rise and have breached the
EUR 100/MWh for the first time since Dec 2022). The Asian gas bench-
mark (JKM) is following the same trend but at a slower pace (4.6% w/w

to USD 32 /MBtu, equivalent to around EUR 96.3 /MWh).
TO WATCH NEXT WEEK
Global: Manufacturing PMIs for EM and final DM prints (Monday); final
services and composite PMIs (Wednesday).
y/y = year-on-year
m/m = month-on-month
bp = basis point
pp = percentage point
mb = million barrels

 政策解读
10 月 16 日，生态环境部对 11 项温室气体自愿减排项目方法学征求意
见。这是 CCER 市场重启以来规模最大的一批。按领域分，绿色化工
与低碳燃料 5 项（可再生能源合成氨、农林生物质气化制甲醇、农林
生物质气化耦合绿氢制甲醇、利用废弃油脂生产烃基生物柴油作为燃
料使用、利用废弃油脂生产酯基生物柴油作为燃料使用），储能 3 项
（抽水蓄能电站、钠离子电池储能电站、全钒液流电池储能电站），
交通运输 1 项（矿山运输用车电动化）、资源循环利用 2 项（低值废
塑料化学再生、厨余垃圾集中厌氧处理沼气工程）。
全国温室气体自愿减排交易市场支持领域扩大，据统计，截至 2025 年
年底，我国已发布 CCER 方法学 18 个，分别是造林碳汇、并网光热
发电、并网海上风力发电、红树林营造、公路隧道照明系统节能、甲

烷体积浓度低于 8%的煤矿低浓度瓦斯和风排瓦斯利用、海上油田伴生
气回收利用、陆上气田试气放喷气回收利用、陆上油田低气量伴生气
回收利用、淤地坝碳汇、滨海盐沼植被修复、海草床植被修、规模化
猪场粪污沼气回收利用工程、农业废弃物集中处理工程、既有公共建
筑围护结构与供暖通风空调系统能效提升、可再生能源电解水制氢、
电气设备六氟化硫回收和净化、中深层地热能井下换热供暖。
全国温室气体自愿减排交易市场登记的核证自愿减排量除用于全国碳
排放权交易市场和碳排放权交易试点市场配额清缴抵销外，还可用于
大型活动碳中和、企业环境社会治理、生态修复等多样场景。2024 年
1 月，全国温室气体自愿减排交易市场正式启动。
根据 2026 年全国碳市场发展报告数据，截至 2026 年 8 月底，全国温
室气体自愿减排交易市场累计登记自愿减排项目 41 个、核证自愿减排
量 2423.35 万吨，核证自愿减排量累计成交量 2170.68 万吨、成交额
17.37 亿元。

### Page 2

ESG 与可持续发展周报
2024 年启动标准前期研究，联合青海大学等科研单位，组建标准编制
组，推进标准研究制定。截至目前，依托绿电溯源成果，青海已建成
全省 992 家企业的“碳账户”，有效支撑“碳账户”贷款发放，推动
绿色金融精准服务低碳企业。
（2）“十五五”时期，全国规划建成 500 个零碳工厂，同时将非化石
能源电力消费物理认定量占比目标提升至 35%。这一政策背景让零碳
工厂成为新型储能最具确定性的应用场景之一。业内人士认为，在政
策硬约束驱动下，储能正从过去单一的峰谷调节工具，升级为支撑绿
电消纳、算电协同、碳资产管理与源网荷储一体化的关键枢纽，配套
储能设施也从工厂建设的可选项加速转变为必选项。与此同时，经济
性瓶颈、长时储能技术缺口、精细化运营能力不足，以及绿色供应链
的国际合规挑战，仍是储能全面落地工厂场景必须跨越的坎。
 技术前沿
《2026 全球碳中和年度进展报告》（以下简称《报告》）近日发布。
《报告》指出，2010—2024 年，全球光伏降本中超过六成、风电降本

中超过八成来自我国，我国风光成本下降对全球产生溢出效应。当
前，我国已形成低碳技术的全球领先优势。2025 年我国新增可再生能
源装机 440 吉瓦，约占全球的 64%，风光度电成本居全球最低区间；
纯电动车销量 820 万辆，保有量占全球的 58.9%。在新兴技术领域，
绿 氢新 增 产能 占 全球 的 70% ， 新型 储 能装 机 136 吉 瓦、 同 比增 长
84%，碳捕集、利用与封存（CCUS）运行项目 20 个、居全球第二，
五年森林生物质碳储量增加约 11.5 亿吨。
《报告》指出，全球低碳转型正进入由技术驱动、系统条件决定的新
阶段。与此同时，风光储、电动车等成熟技术加速发展，但电网消纳
能力、融资、市场机制等方面存在的不足仍制约着市场替代；清洁
氢、CCUS 等新兴技术仍面临需求、基础设施和商业模式不足的问
题。
 研究探索
10 月 20 日，在由中国金融四十人论坛（CF40）与清华大学（THU）
联合主办的 2026 外滩年会上，2026 外滩绿色金融报告（下称报告）

正式发布。本次报告主题为“绿色低碳协同发展及金融支持研究”。
中国人民银行研究局局长、CF40 成员王信担任课题负责人。报告跳出
“就绿色论绿色”的传统视角，提出“协同发展”应对多重目标、多
元风险叠加复杂格局的路径：第一层是绿色低碳发展自身的协同，包
括气候减缓与气候适应的协同、应对气候变化与自然生态保护的协
同；第二层是绿色低碳转型与国内宏观经济金融的协同；第三层是国
际视角下的协同。
报告选取农业、城市、健康医疗三个代表性领域，测算“十五五”期
间气候适应投资规模。预计“十五五”期间，农业领域气候适应投资
总需求约 1.05 万亿元，年均约 2100 亿元；39 个深化气候适应型城市
（区）建设试点地区气候适应总投资需求约 3000 亿至 4000 亿元，年
均约 600 亿至 800 亿元，扩展到全国，年均投资需求规模约 4000 亿
至 5400 亿元；健康医疗领域气候适应投资总需求约 1.88 万亿元，年
均约 3800 亿元。绿色消费方面，2025 年我国绿色消费市场规模达

12.18 万亿元，占社零总额 24.3%。报告预计，2030 年我国绿色消费
将突破 18 万亿元，占比提升至 30%左右。
王信表示，需要完善高级别政策协同机制，逐步把气候、自然等因素
纳入国民经济发展战略、财政管理框架、货币政策框架以及宏观审慎

Great improvement in 3Q26

Intel reports 3Q26 sales of US$16.5bn, 2% qoq and 21% yoy, in line
with Bloomberg estimates. 3Q26 gross margin of 43% was 2ppts
higher than Bloomberg estimates. 3Q26 EPS of US$0.6 is much
higher than Bloomberg’s US$0.28 largely due to 2ppts better gross
margin, 4ppts better operating margin and non-operating income of
US$1.1bn.
Data center IC sales beat: 3Q26 Data center IC sales (largely
server CPU sales) were US$7.32bn, up 17% qoq and 78% yoy,
nearly 10% above consensus estimates. Data center IC sales

accounted for 44% of 3Q26 sales, up from 2Q26’s 39% and from
3Q25’s 30%. Operating margin for data center IC sales was 42%,
much higher than Bloomberg’s 27%, and improved from 2Q26’s
40% and 3Q25’s 23%.
Improve in progress for foundry business: In addition to
stronger data center IC sales, Intel foundry 3Q26 sales improved to
US$6.7bn, up 16% qoq and 58% yoy. Driven by higher utilization
rate, 3Q26 operating margin for Intel foundry was improved to -28%

### Page 2

from 2Q26’s -36% and 3Q25’s -55%, which was much better than
Bloomberg’s -36% estimates.
Source: Intel, Bloomberg est.

### Page 3

Source: Andrew Lu on global semis/techs
4Q26 guidance missing on sales, margin, and EPS
Combined eﬀects from lack of demand on client computing (due to
memory price hike and PC customers to aggressively manage
inventory might extend to 1H27) and lack of data center CPU
capacity (limited by in house capacity after Israel Kiryat Gat fab
factory shut down), Intel guides 4Q26 sales of US$15-16bn, down 3-
9% qoq and 9% below Bloomberg estimate of US$17bn (Fab shut

down aﬀects 2ppts sales). 4Q26 gross margin is guided to
deteriorate to 37% (Fab shut down aﬀects 2ppts margin), 6ppts
lower than 3Q26’s 43% and Bloomberg’s 42.8%, resulting much
lower diluted EPS of US$0.1 (Bloomberg’s US$0.3).

## Document 387: LatAm’s silicon dividend_ tailwinds from the AI investment boom in the U.S. (2026-10-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/LatAm’s silicon dividend_ tailwinds from the AI  investment boom in the U.S. (2026-10-28).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

LatAm’s silicon dividend: 
tailwinds from the AI  
investment boom in the U.S.

1 
Authors 
Fancisco Campos 
Chief LatAm Economist

Beatriz Garcia Nunes 
LatAm Economist

October 28, 2026 
Key messages: 
- U.S. investment has broken away from Europe and China since 2023, 
powered by AI-related equipment, software and research spending. 
- The AI CapEx boom demands commodities and merchandise that LatAm 
can supply.  
- Latin America’s export gains are real but concentrated. Copper, computer 
and electronics products provide the clearest links to the U.S. AI buildout. 
- Chile is the region’s most direct copper beneficiary. Mexico captures the 
windfall stemming from increases hardware demand and trade-diversion. In 
these cases, the AI-related tailwinds have undergirded appreciable 
improvements in external accounts. 
- The cases for nickel and zinc illustrate that the dividend is not widespread. 
U.S. imports of the former have mostly returned toward pre-pandemic levels, 
while for the latter bilateral gains partly reflect supplier substitution. 
- While expanding AI use, supply shortages and incentives can extend the 
CapEx boom, its financial viability poses downside risks. 
Another ‘American exceptionalism’ story  
Investment has become one of the sharpest dividing lines among the world’s 
core economic blocs. Since 2024, U.S. gross fixed capital formation has firmed 
up appreciably. Meanwhile, investment in Europe has broadly stalled while in 
China’s it has retreated, the drivers of which are beyond the scope of this report 
(Figure 1).  
The U.S. divergence has largely been an AI story: a burst of spending on 
computing power, digital infrastructure and intellectual property has offset 
weakness in conventional construction and housing. Indeed, total U.S. fixed 
investment is nearly 12% above its 2023 level. Within it, investment in 
information-processing equipment has risen by roughly 57%, software outlays by 
about 35%, and research and development by around 15%. By contrast, non-
residential structures and residential investment sit below their 2023 levels 
(Figure 2).

### Page 2

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 2

Figure 1: The global investment divergence

Figure 2: AI-related categories lead U.S. investment

Source: US Bureau of Economic Analysis, Eurostat, National Bureau of Statistics of 
China, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Source: US Bureau of Economic Analysis, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Latin America’s commodity channel 
AI is not merely a semiconductor story. Chips require specialized minerals; data 
centers require electricity and cooling; electricity requires grids, transformers and 
storage. That chain pulls in copper, aluminum, silver, tin, lithium and selected 
semiconductor inputs, along with natural gas and uranium. Latin America has 
meaningful exposure to several of these inputs, but copper has emerged as the 
commodity with the cleanest and most economically significant regional link. 
Copper is used throughout the AI infrastructure stack, including power cables, 
substations, transformers, server boards, heat sinks and cooling systems. Rising 
U.S. copper demand has lifted imports from Chile and Peru, particularly the 
former (Figure 3). T he effect matters beyond merchandise export volumes. 
Favorable terms-of-trade have helped Chile generate a current -account surplus 
for the first time in roughly fifteen years (0.2% of GDP as of Q2 2026 on a 4 -
quarter rolling basis ) and have allowed Peru to log the largest surplus in about 
forty-five years (4.5% of GDP as of Q2 2026 on a 4-quarter rolling basis).

28 October 2026

### Page 3

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 3

Figure 3: Copper epitomized the commodity link

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Not every metal is copper 
The broader mining story demands more caution, though. U.S. nickel imports have 
recovered toward pre-Covid levels, but the upswing has not displayed the same 
persistence as copper. Mexico has captured a disproportionate share of the 
bilateral improvement, yet that gain looks more like normalization than a 
sustained AI -driven break (Figure 4). Zinc tells a similar story. U.S. real imports 
stand shy of their pre-pandemic levels, and stronger purchases from Mexico and 
Peru partly reflect a reallocation away from Brazil rather than a decisive increase 
in U.S. demand (Figure 5).

Figure 4: Nickel: recovery, not a boom

Figure 5: Zinc: a substitution story

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

28 October 2026

### Page 4

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 4

Mexico’s hardware advantage 
The larger non-commodity prize is in computers and electronics. U.S. imports of 
these products have sky -rocketed, while the supplier mix has shifted away from 
China. Mexico and various Asian economies have gained share on the back of this 
double-whammy impulse (Figure 6). 
This helps explain why Mexico’s non-automotive manufacturing exports, notably 
electronics, have outpaced automotive shipments to the U.S. and pressures from 
higher oil prices. The resulting non -oil trade surplus has offset pressure from the 
oil balance and helped Mexico generate a balanced current account (0% of GDP 
as of Q2 2026 on a 4 -quarter rolling basis). The external boost is meaningful, 
although it sho uld not necessarily be interpreted as bona fide  evidence of 
nearshoring, in our view.

Figure 6: U.S. computer & electronics imports going into overdrive

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research.

How much runway remains? 
Quantifying or mapping out a timeframe for the AI investment outlook is beyond 
the scope of this piece or our expertise. Nevertheless, we’d venture to argue that 
the capex cycle appears more likely to mature gradually than to stop abruptly . 
One reason is that the investment case is shifting from frontier -model training 
toward enterprise inference . Capacity that once served development can 
increasingly run commercial workloads for companies. That creates a path to 
revenue even if artificial general intelligence (AGI) crystallizes later than 
expected. It also spreads demand to a much larger corporate customer base.  
A second reason is scarcity. Graphics processing units (GPUs) remain tight, while 
power availability, grid connections, cooling equipment, construction capacity 
and data -center complexity limit how quickly supply can respond. Bottlenecks 
may therefore exte nd the cycle. The constraint is especially relevant for copper 
28 October 2026

### Page 5

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 5

and power infrastructure, where projects have long lead times and permitting 
hurdles. 
A third reason is strategic. AI development resembles a prisoner’s dilemma at 
both the corporate and national levels. Each participant may worry about costs, 
safety and control, yet slowing unilaterally risks surrendering technological, 
commercial or natio nal security advantages. The same tension is visible in calls 
for guardrails alongside continued spending. Competition can therefore sustain 
capex even when short-term returns are uncertain. 
We acknowledge the risks, nonetheless. Higher borrowing costs raise the hurdle 
rate for an infrastructure buildout financed partly through debt. More important, 
revenues must eventually justify the installed capital base. The bearish case is not 
that AI la cks value; it is that infrastructure spending may run ahead of 
monetization, compromising some companies’ repayment capacity. Public 
opposition to the development of AI infrastructure and/or adoption of AI 
technologies could also slow down the technology’s roll-out. 
The regional balance  
Summing up, the AI investment boom in the U .S. is delivering a dividend to parts 
of Latin America, not the region as a whole. Chile and Peru benefit through copper 
prices and export volumes. Mexico benefits through electronics manufacturing 
and the replacement of Chinese supply. For Brazil and Colombia, the AI dividend 
is rather muted. Where materialized, furthermore, the dividend supports trade 
and current accounts more clearly rather than improving the domestic 
investment. 
These dynamics should persist while U.S. AI investment remains supply -
constrained and strategically protected. If widespread adoption advances and 
the strategic race intensifies, the impulse can last. If financing costs rise further 
or monetization disappo ints, the cycle may narrow. Either way, copper and 
Mexican electronics remain the region’s most visible gauges of the U.S. AI 
investment appetite.

28 October 2026

### Page 6

Deutsche Bank Research Institute 
LatAm’s silicon dividend: tailwinds from the AI investment boom in the U.S. – October 28, 2026

### Page 2

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The monthly household spending indicator rose 0.6%mth in
September (nominal), exceeding both Westpac and market
expectations. We had anticipated a 0.5%mth contraction
(on the basis of a softer read on vehicle sales in the month),

while the market had pencilled in a 0.2%mth rise. Today’s
result instead marks a return to growth following August’s
brief contraction (revised down 0.1ppt to –0.1%mth), with
annual growth strengthening to 6.8%yr. For Q3 as a whole,
nominal spending rose 2.1%qtr, the strongest quarterly
result since the December quarter of 2022. In real terms,
volumes increased a more subdued 0.7%qtr, pointing to a
sharp acceleration in price growth through the quarter.

Non-discretionary spending outperforms
By broad category, services spending more than reversed
last month’s decline, rising a solid 1.1%mth (+6.8%yr). Goods
spending held around its recent pace, increasing 0.3%mth
(+6.7%yr). Discretionary spending returned to growth in
September, rising 0.3%mth (+6.9%yr), although this failed to
fully unwind August’s decline.
The relative strength in non-discretionary spending observed
last month continued into September, with the category rising

1.2%mth (+6.5%yr). While the headline result was stronger,
the breakdown aligns with findings from our Westpac–DataX
Card Tracker, and Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel  which
show discretionary goods continuing to underperform other
categories (see here and here).
At the detailed level, transport and health led the gains,
both rising 1.5%mth. The increase in transport spending was
largely attributable to higher fuel spending, up 5.4%mth,
reflecting elevated fuel prices, with volumes down 2.2%mth.

The ABS also noted higher spending on vehicle repairs
and maintenance, while domestic air travel increased as
households travelled to attend major sporting events.
Recreation & culture rose 0.8%mth, while miscellaneous
goods & services (+0.6%mth), hotels, cafes & restaurants
(+0.5%mth), and clothing & footwear (+0.1%mth) also
recorded gains.
Partially offsetting these increases, food spending declined
0.1%mth, while furnishings & household equipment fell

0.2%mth, likely reflecting the ongoing weakness in housing
turnover (see here). Alcoholic beverages & tobacco recorded
the largest decline, falling 0.7%mth.
State outcomes were positive across the board in September.
WA led the gains, rising 1.1%mth and reversing last
month’s decline. Victoria also rebounded (+1.0%mth),
while Queensland (+0.6%mth), SA (+0.6%mth), Tasmania
(+0.5%mth) and NSW (+0.3%mth) all recorded increases.

are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Volume growth remains firm 
Nominal spending rose a firm 2.1%qtr in Q3, while volumes 
increased a more subdued 0.7%qtr, matching the pace recorded 
in Q2. This implies a sharp acceleration in price growth to 
1.4%qtr, from 0.6%qtr in Q2, and marks the strongest quarterly 
increase in prices since the December quarter of 2022. Through 
the year, volume growth strengthened to 3.2%yr, remaining 
well above the 1.8%yr average recorded through 2025.
Transport led gains in volume terms, rising 1.6%qtr, 
likely partly reflecting base effects in air travel following 
disruptions associated with the Middle East conflict in the June 
quarter. Clothing & footwear (+1.2%qtr), health (+1.1%qtr), 
miscellaneous goods & services (+1.1%qtr), food (+0.5%qtr), 
recreation & culture (+0.5%qtr), and furnishings & household 
equipment (+0.1%qtr) also recorded increases. Hotels, cafes 
& restaurants spending declined 0.1%qtr in volume terms, 
while alcoholic beverages & tobacco was the main drag, falling 
1.2%qtr.
Most states recorded real spending growth over the quarter, 
led by WA (+1.4%qtr), Victoria (+0.9%qtr), NSW (+0.5%qtr), 
Queensland (+0.5%qtr) and SA (+0.4%qtr). Tasmania lagged its 
state peers, recording a 0.1%qtr decline.
Conclusion
The household spending indicator provides a useful guide to 
around two-thirds of total consumer expenditure. Importantly, 
it excludes several major categories included in the National 
Accounts measure of real household consumption, including 
rents and utilities.

Today’s volumes outcome was broadly in line with our 
expectations, with Westpac anticipating around a 0.6%qtr rise 
in Q3, and remains consistent with our view on consumption 
ahead of the Q3 National Accounts (due 2 December). 
However, this will likely be the high water mark for spending. 
The renewed deterioration in global energy markets and the 
cumulative effect of four rate increases over the first three 
quarters of the year are likely to weigh more heaveily on 
household spending in Q4.

Looking further ahead, households will continue to face 
a restrictive monetary policy environment, with the RBA 
delivering an additional rate increase at yesterday’s November 
meeting (see here). Combined with persistent consumer 
pessimism, ongoing cost-of-living pressures (see here) and a 
housing downturn impacting household wealth, spending looks 
set to slow further in 2027.

## Document 429: The Commodities Feed_ Oil plunges following US midterm elections

Source PDF: all report pdf/The Commodities Feed_ Oil plunges following US midterm elections.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Energy markets are under signiﬁcant pressure following the
Democrats’ clean sweep in the US midterms as hopes grow
that the political shift could accelerate eﬀorts to bring the war
with Iran to an end

Warren Patterson and Ewa Manthey
Energy – Middle East optimism grows
following US election
Oil markets sold oﬀ aggressively yesterday, with ICE Brent settling
8.7% lower on the day and below $95/bbl. Selling pressure has only
continued in early morning trading today. The outcome of the US
midterm elections, with the Democrats winning both the House and
Senate has increased hopes that we could see a quicker end to the
war in Iran. Given the Democrats’ control Congress, there will likely
be more scrutiny over the war, while funding it could become more
diﬃcult. The outcome of the elections means there could be more
pushback from the Republican Party, too. Yet the new senators and

### Page 2

members of the House start in January, leaving room for President
Trump to escalate if he wants. Trump has suggested there are no
imminent plans to strike Iran.
How all this plays out is still highly uncertain. Therefore, the degree
and speed to which the oil market has sold oﬀ seems to be
overdone. One would think Trump might want to come away with
some accomplishments from this war. Does he go about that
through renewed strikes or striking a deal? Or by continuing to put

pressure on Iran with the ongoing maritime blockade? In the lead
up to the midterms, all signals pointed towards Trump resuming
strikes. This is less clear now.
In the absence of escalation, the oil market is likely to continue to
slowly give back the risk premium it built in recent months, with oil
ﬂows from the Persian Gulf holding up well. This risk premium could
disappear even quicker if there’s some sort of agreement between
the US and Iran. Fundamentally, the oil market is looking

increasingly softer, reﬂected in the weakening in the physical
market as well as timespreads.
Inventory data from the EIA yesterday showed that US commercial
crude oil inventories fell by 865k barrels over the last week. When
including releases from strategic reserves total crude, inventories
fell by 1.26m barrels. Meanwhile, changes on the reﬁned product
side were even more supportive, particularly for middle distillates.
Despite reﬁneries increasing their utilisation rates by 1.7 percentage

points over the week to 94.5%, distillate fuel oil inventories still fell
by 3.57m barrels. This took total distillate stocks below 100m
barrels for the ﬁrst time since 2003. Gasoline inventories also edged
lower by 222k barrels.
European gas prices also sold oﬀ yesterday amid hopes the US
midterms outcome could lead to a quicker recovery in LNG ﬂows.
TTF fell by more than 3.8% and pressure has continued this
morning, leaving it trading around the EUR85/MWh level. However,

fundamentally, the European gas market remains tight. Storage
has started drawing with the heating season now underway. EU
storage stands at 74% full. Where we come out the other side of

winter is largely dependent on how much of a recovery we see in
Persian Gulf LNG ﬂows this winter, if any. And, of course, given the

tighter market, weather is going to be even more important than
usual through the winter.
Metals – Gold surges back above $4,000/oz
Spot gold jumped more than 3% yesterday, climbing back above
$4,000/oz following a Democratic sweep in the US mid-term
elections. Expectations of legislative gridlock, lower Treasury yields
and a weaker dollar are supporting the move.
Meanwhile, central bank buying continues to provide underlying
support, with oﬃcial-sector demand remaining strong in

September. Central banks added 61 tonnes during the month,
according to the World Gold Council, while third-quarter purchases
reached 228 tonnes (-7% year-on-year). Poland and China
continued to lead purchases, reinforcing the structural demand
story for gold.
Despite the rebound, the near-term outlook remains challenging for
the precious metal. Inﬂation concerns continue to support
expectations for tighter monetary policy. But much depends on
developments in the Middle East and what they mean for energy

markets.

## Document 447: Triển vọng TTCK Việt Nam 4Q2026 (2026-10-07)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Triển vọng TTCK Việt Nam 4Q2026 (2026-10-07).pdf
Report source: KBSV (Strategy)
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 27
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Trần Đức Anh 
Giám đốc Kinh tế Vĩ mô & Chiến lược thị trường 
anhtd@kbsec.com.vn 
07/10/2026

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 điểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 điểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao 
phục vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức 
cao cho phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện 
đáng kể trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm 
phán vẫn đang bế tắc và ít có khả năng dòng chảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối 
lại ngay trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi lựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đầu tư công, chính sách tiền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

I. Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
TTCK Việt Nam đi ngang, giảm nhẹ 
trong 9 tháng đầu năm 
 TTCK Việt Nam biến động giằng co, giảm điểm nhẹ trong 9 tháng đầu năm với 
diễn biến có thể chia làm 2 giai đoạn chính. Giai đoạn 1 kéo dài đến gần cuối 
quý 1, thị trường đi ngang trong 2 tháng đầu, trước khi lao dốc mạnh từ cuối 
tháng 2 trước ảnh hưởng bởi xung đột tại Trung Đông bùng phát. Giai đoạn 2 là 
khoảng thời gian còn lại, thị trường xuất hiện các nhịp biến động mạnh, 
tăng/giảm đan xen, chịu ảnh hưởng bởi thông tin về giá dầu, biến động lãi suất 
trong nước, kỳ vọng về nâng hạng thị trường và các yếu tố cơ bản khác. Tính 
chung 9 tháng, VNIndex gần như đi ngang, giảm nhẹ 0.9% so với cuối 2025, 
trong khi thanh khoản bình quân toàn thị trường giảm xấp xỉ 12% so với cùng 
kỳ.

Với diễn biến căng thẳng tại Trung Đông vẫn đang dai dẳng, đồng thời dự báo 
mặt bằng lãi suất sẽ khó xuất hiện xu hướng hạ nhiệt rõ nét trong năm 2026, dù

Lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm 
yết dự báo tăng trưởng mạnh

Chúng tôi điều chỉnh dự báo tăng trưởng EPS toàn thị trường cho năm 2026 lên 
mức 33% YoY, chủ yếu nhờ phản ánh mức tăng 200% kế hoạch lợi nhuận sau 
thuế của VIC (loại trừ mức tăng trưởng đột biến này của VIC, tăng trưởng EPS 
toàn thị trường ở mức 17% YoY). Đóng góp chính đến từ mức tăng của một số 
nhóm có tỷ trọng lớn: Bất động sản (+79% YoY), Nguyên vật liệu (+44% YoY) và 
Hàng tiêu dùng thiết yếu (+27% YoY). Mức tăng trưởng EPS cao, tương đồng với 
số liệu tăng trưởng kinh tế vĩ mô tích cực, là động lực quan trọng hỗ trợ thị 
trường trong trung/dài hạn.

Bối cảnh vĩ mô quốc tế còn nhiều 
bất định

Bối cảnh kinh tế toàn cầu trong giai đoạn cuối năm vẫn chịu áp lực lớn từ xung 
đột địa chính trị ở Trung Đông và động thái điều hành chính sách của Fed. Đàm 
phán Mỹ – Iran chưa đạt tiến triển đáng kể, trong khi giao tranh tiếp diễn và 
hoạt động vận chuyển dầu bị gián đoạn, làm gia tăng rủi ro thiếu hụt nguồn 
cung và khiến giá dầu được dự báo tiếp tục neo cao trong thời gian tới. Giá 
năng lượng duy trì ở mức cao, cùng với áp lực lạm phát chưa hạ nhiệt rõ rệt, 
khiến Fed tiếp tục duy trì lập trường thận trọng và không loại trừ khả năng tiếp 
tục thắt chặt chính sách tiền tệ trong quý 4.

Mặt bằng lãi suất huy động dự báo 
duy trì mức cao trong quý 4

Chúng tôi duy trì đánh giá tích cực về triển vọng tăng trưởng kinh tế Việt Nam 
năm 2026, với GDP dự kiến tăng 8.5–9.0%, dựa trên kỳ vọng cán cân thương 
mại cải thiện, dòng vốn FDI tiếp tục hỗ trợ tăng trưởng và đầu tư công được đẩy 
mạnh. Tuy nhiên, giá năng lượng là rủi ro đáng chú ý, có thể gây thêm áp lực 
lên cán cân thương mại và lạm phát, qua đó ảnh hưởng đến triển vọng tăng 
trưởng trong thời gian tới. 
Áp lực thiếu hụt thanh khoản ngắn hạn đã có dấu hiệu hạ nhiệt, trong khi áp 
lực huy động vốn trung và dài hạn vẫn hiện hữu khi nhu cầu vốn của nền kinh 
tế dự kiến ở mức cao. Do đó, nhìn về cuối năm, chúng tôi cho rằng lãi suất chưa 
thể xuất hiện xu hướng giảm rõ rệt, nhưng kỳ vọng có thể ổn định quanh mặt 
bằng hiện tại nhờ hoạt động giải ngân đầu tư công tiếp tục hỗ trợ dòng tiền và 
NHNN vẫn còn dư địa điều tiết thanh khoản trong bối cảnh áp lực tỷ giá không 
lớn và áp lực lạm phát vẫn trong tầm kiểm soát.

Biểu đồ 5. P/E forward 2026 các thị trường  Biểu đồ 6. Biến động EPS nhóm ASEAN4

Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV  Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV

Định giá chỉ số VNIndex đang ở 
vùng hấp dẫn 
 P/E của chỉ số VNIndex hiện đang ở mức 13.5 (theo Bloomberg), thấp hơn đáng 
kể so với mức bình quân 5 năm gần nhất ở 15.1.  
Nếu loại trừ mức tăng đột biến ở nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup từ đầu năm 2025 đến 
nay, P/E của thị trường (sau đây gọi là P/E điều chỉnh) chỉ còn ở mức 10.9 lần. Đây 
là mức thấp hơn cả các đáy gần nhất trong quá khứ như đáy Covid vào tháng 
3/2020 (ở 11.3 lần), đáy của năm 2022 (ở 11.01 lần), và đáy thuế quan trong 
năm 2025 (ở 12 lần).

Lưu ý: chúng tôi tính toán P/E điều chỉnh với giả định P/E nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup 
giữ nguyên từ đầu năm 2025 (hay nói cách khác, biến động giá ở nhóm cổ 
phiếu này tương đồng với lợi nhuận trượt 4 quý). Cách tính này giúp loại bỏ ảnh 
hưởng của nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup lên VNIndex, khi mà riêng 2 cổ phiếu VIC, 
VHM đã đóng góp xấp xỉ 450 điểm cho mức tăng của chỉ số VNIndex từ đầu năm 
2025, và P/E của VIC hiện đang giao dịch ở mức 80 lần.

Chỉ số VNIndex được dự báo ở 
quanh mức 1,800 điểm thời điểm 
cuối năm 2026 
 Đối với triển vọng thị trường trong quý cuối năm 2026, với đánh giá mặt bằng 
lãi suất sẽ tiếp tục duy trì ở mức cao, kết hợp với xu hướng tăng trưởng lợi 
nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết vẫn sẽ được duy trì, chúng tôi dự báo vùng 
điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 1,800 điểm, tương 
đương mức tăng gần 2% so với đóng cửa quý 3. Dự báo này tương ứng:

- Mức tăng trưởng EPS 2026 của thị trường chung được kỳ vọng tăng 
mạnh lên mức 33% (tăng so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ 
yếu nhờ kỳ vọng vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và 
lợi nhuận tăng trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt 
động kinh doanh).

- Về mặt định giá, chúng tôi giảm nhẹ vùng P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIn-
dex thời điểm cuối năm xuống 13.2 (từ mức 13.5 ở thời điểm hiện tại - 
phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là vùng 
định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm, và vùng thấp lịch sử nếu loại bỏ 
đà tăng đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup từ đầu năm 2025 (thể hiện ở chỉ số 
P/E điều chỉnh mà chúng tôi nhận định ở trên). Đây là cơ sở để chúng tôi 
kỳ vọng thị trường sẽ có nhịp bật tăng mạnh ngay khi môi trường vĩ mô 
quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran) và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn đề lãi 
suất) chuyển biến theo chiều hướng tích cực. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi cho 
rằng các cải thiện này sẽ cần chờ đến nửa đầu 2027, vì vậy chúng tôi 
chưa nâng mức P/E hợp lý của thị trường ngay trong báo cáo này.
